    box("News Agent", "AI", k && k.news ? "Score " + k.news.score + ", confidence " + k.news.confidence + ", " + k.news.headlines.length + " headlines" : ""),
    box("Red-team Agent", "AI", v ? v.n_vetoes + " vetoes, " + v.good_vetoes + " right, net effect " + inr(v.net_effect_inr) : ""),
    box("Reflection Agent", "AI", lz ? lz.verdict + ": " + lz.lesson : ""),
    box("Bull, Bear and Judge", "AI", db ? "Judge: " + db.judge.verdict + ". " + db.judge.reason : ""),
    box("Regime Agent", "Code", rg ? rg.label + " (x" + rg.conf_mult + "). " + rg.why : ""),
    box("Dynamic Risk Agent", "Code", rk ? "Size x" + rk.scale + ". " + rk.why.join("; ") : ""),
    box("Portfolio Agent", "Code", pf ? Object.entries(pf.weights).map(e => e[0] + " " + Math.round(e[1] * 100) + "%").join(", ") + ". " + pf.flag : ""),
    box("Drift Monitor", "ML", dr ? dr.status + ": accuracy " + dr.accuracy + ", coverage " + dr.coverage : ""),
    box("C++ Risk Gate", "Hard rules", k ? (k.gate_failed && k.gate_failed.length ? "Blocked by: " + k.gate_failed.join(", ") : "All gates passed") + (k.audit_ok ? ". Audit chain OK" : "") : "")
  ];
  return `<h3>Market</h3>${sig}<div class="c"><div class="row"><b>NIFTY 50</b><span class="k">${last ? last.close.toLocaleString("en-IN") : "-"}</span><span class="${cl(chg)}">${chg.toFixed(2)}%</span><small>NIFTYBEES last ${inr(S.acct.lastPrice || 0)}</small></div>${chart(c)}</div><h3>Agents</h3><div class="gr">` + boxes.join("") + `</div>`;
}

function advise(kind) {
  const a = S.acct, px = a.lastPrice || 0, ag = (S.mk && S.mk.a) || {}, k = S.mk && S.mk.t && S.mk.t.card;
  const pos = (a.holdings || {}).NIFTYBEES, eq = a.equity ?? a.cash;
  const rk = ag.risk ? ag.risk.scale : 1, rm = ag.regime ? ag.regime.conf_mult : 1;
  const gates = (k && k.gate_failed) || [];
  const hard = gates.filter(g => ["vix_stress", "kill_switch", "data_invalid", "rate_limit"].includes(g));
  const judge = ag.debate ? ag.debate.judge.verdict : null;
  const pctSize = S.prefs.sizePct || 5;
  const why = [];
  let qty = 0, stance = "", tone = "mu";
  if (!px) return {stance: "No price yet", qty: 0, why: ["Waiting for the next market check."], tone};
  if (kind === "BUY") {
    const full = Math.floor(eq * (pctSize / 100) * rk * rm / px), dir = k ? k.ml_direction : null;
    why.push(`Full size = ${pctSize}% of equity x risk ${rk} x regime ${rm} = ${full} units.`);
    if (hard.length) { stance = "AVOID"; tone = "n"; why.push("Safety gate active: " + hard.join(", ") + "."); }
    else if (dir === "SELL") { stance = "AGAINST SIGNAL"; tone = "n"; qty = Math.floor(full * 0.2); why.push("Model leans SELL today. If you still buy, keep it tiny."); }
    else if (k && k.decision === "TRADE" && dir === "BUY") { stance = "AGREE"; tone = "p"; qty = full; why.push("Model says BUY and all gates passed."); }
    else { stance = "WEAK, WAIT"; qty = Math.floor(full * 0.4); why.push("No clear edge today (" + (gates.join(", ") || "no signal") + ")."); }
    if (judge === "WAIT" && qty) { qty = Math.floor(qty * 0.5); why.push("Debate judge says WAIT, so size is halved."); }
    qty = Math.max(0, Math.min(qty, Math.floor(a.cash / (px * 1.002))));
    if (qty) why.push(`If you buy: stop-loss near ${inr(px * 0.98)} (-2%), target near ${inr(px * 1.03)} (+3%), exit within 5 days.`);
  } else {
    if (!pos || !pos.qty) return {stance: "NOTHING TO SELL", qty: 0, tone: "n", why: ["You hold no NIFTYBEES. Short selling is not supported."]};
    const pnl = (px / pos.avg - 1) * 100, days = pos.since ? Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(pos.since)) / 864e5) : 0;
    why.push(`You hold ${pos.qty} at ${inr(pos.avg)}. Now ${pnl.toFixed(2)}%, held ${days} day(s).`);
    if (pnl <= -2) { stance = "STOP-LOSS HIT"; tone = "n"; qty = pos.qty; why.push("Down 2% or more: cut the loss."); }
    else if (pnl >= 3) { stance = "TARGET HIT"; tone = "p"; qty = pos.qty; why.push("Up 3% or more: book the profit."); }
    else if (days >= 5) { stance = "TIME EXIT"; qty = pos.qty; why.push("Held 5 days or more: close it."); }
    else if (k && k.ml_direction === "SELL" && k.decision === "TRADE") { stance = "SIGNAL SAYS SELL"; tone = "p"; qty = pos.qty; why.push("Model says SELL and gates passed."); }
    else if (k && k.ml_direction === "SELL") { stance = "LEAN SELL"; qty = Math.ceil(pos.qty / 2); why.push("Model leans SELL but no clear edge: trim half."); }
    else { stance = "HOLD"; why.push("No stop, target or sell signal yet."); }
  }
  why.push("Round-trip costs are about 0.15%, so tiny moves lose money.");
  return {stance, qty, why, tone};
}

function draw() {
    if (!U) return;
    const a = S.acct;
    if (!a) {
      m.innerHTML = `<div class="c" style="max-width:420px"><b>Open your paper account</b><p class="mu">You get ₹10,00,000 of fake money. Choose a name and a PIN. The PIN is needed for every payment and order.</p>
        ${U.emailVerified ? `<label class="mu">Your name</label><input id="nm" maxlength="40" autocomplete="name" style="width:100%">
        <label class="mu">PIN (4 to 6 digits)</label><input id="p1" type="password" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="6" style="width:100%">
        <label class="mu">Repeat PIN</label><input id="p2" type="password" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="6" style="width:100%">
        <p><button class="pr" id="open">Open account</button> <small id="er" class="n"></small></p>
        <small class="mu">There is no PIN reset. A forgotten PIN means a new account. This is a demo PIN, not a bank PIN.</small>` : '<p class="n">Verify your email first on the alerts page, then come back.</p>'}</div>`;
      return;
    }
    const px = a.lastPrice || 0, hs = Object.entries(a.holdings || {});
    const eq = a.equity ?? a.cash, pnl = eq - a.startCash, real = a.realized || 0;
    m.innerHTML = `<div class="c me"><div class="av">${esc((a.name || "?")[0].toUpperCase())}</div>
      <div><b style="font-size:18px">${esc(a.name)}</b><br><small>${esc(U.email)} · ID VTS-${U.uid.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}</small></div>
      <div class="row" style="margin-left:auto"><button class="pr" data-d="fund">Add money</button><button class="buy" data-d="BUY">Buy</button><button class="sell" data-d="SELL">Sell</button></div></div>
    <div class="gr" style="margin-top:10px">
      <div class="c"><div class="k">${inr(eq)}</div><small>Total value</small></div>
      <div class="c"><div class="k">${inr(a.cash)}</div><small>Available balance</small></div>
      <div class="c"><div class="k ${cl(pnl)}">${inr(pnl)}</div><small>Profit or loss (${(pnl / a.startCash * 100).toFixed(2)}% of ${inr(a.startCash)} added)</small></div>
      <div class="c"><div class="k ${cl(real)}">${inr(real)}</div><small>Booked profit or loss</small></div></div>
    ${S.pend.length ? "<h3>Agent needs your approval</h3>" + S.pend.map(p => `<div class="c pd"><div class="row"><b>${esc(p.side)} ${p.qty} ${esc(p.symbol)}</b><span class="mu">at about ${inr(p.price)}, confidence ${p.conf}</span></div><p>${esc(p.reason)}</p>
      <div class="row"><button class="buy" data-id="${esc(p.id)}" data-s="approved">Approve</button><button class="no" data-id="${esc(p.id)}" data-s="rejected">Reject</button><small>Expires ${dt(p.expiresAt)}</small></div></div>`).join("") : ""}
    ${market()}<h3>Holdings</h3><div class="c x">${hs.length ? `<table><tr><th>Symbol<th>Qty<th>Avg cost<th>Last<th>Unrealised<th>Since</tr>` + hs.map(([k, h]) => { const u = (px - h.avg) * h.qty; return `<tr><td>${esc(k)}<td>${h.qty}<td>${inr(h.avg)}<td>${inr(px)}<td class="${cl(u)}">${inr(u)}<td>${esc(h.since)}</tr>`; }).join("") + "</table>" : '<span class="mu">No open position.</span>'}</div>
    <h3>Your orders</h3><div class="c x">${S.ords.length ? `<table><tr><th>When<th>Side<th>Qty<th>Status<th></tr>` + S.ords.map(o => `<tr><td>${dt(o.createdAt)}<td>${esc(o.side)}<td>${o.qty}<td><span class="st ${stc(o.status)}">${esc(o.status)}</span> ${esc(o.note || "")}<td>${o.status === "placed" ? `<button class="no" data-oc="${esc(o.id)}">Cancel</button>` : ""}</tr>`).join("") + "</table><small class='mu'>Orders run at the next market check, within 30 minutes in market hours.</small>" : '<span class="mu">No orders yet.</span>'}</div>
    <h3>Payments (simulated)</h3><div class="c x">${S.funds.length ? `<table><tr><th>When<th>Method<th>Amount<th>Status<th>Ref</tr>` + S.funds.map(f => `<tr><td>${dt(f.createdAt)}<td>${esc(f.method)}<td>${inr(f.amount)}<td><span class="st ${stc(f.status)}">${esc(f.status)}</span> ${esc(f.note || "")}<td>${esc(f.txn || "")}</tr>`).join("") + "</table>" : '<span class="mu">No payments yet.</span>'}</div>
    <h3>Value over time</h3><div class="c">${curve(S.eq.map(e => e.equity))}</div>
    <h3>Trade history</h3><div class="c x">${S.trades.length ? `<table><tr><th>When<th>Side<th>Qty<th>Price<th>Fees<th>Profit or loss<th>Why</tr>` + S.trades.map(t => `<tr><td>${dt(t.ts)}<td>${esc(t.side)}<td>${t.qty}<td>${inr(t.price)}<td>${inr(t.fee)}<td class="${t.pnl == null ? "mu" : cl(t.pnl)}">${t.pnl == null ? "open" : inr(t.pnl)}<td>${esc(t.source)}: ${esc(t.reason)}</tr>`).join("") + "</table>" : '<span class="mu">No trades yet.</span>'}</div>
    <h3>Agent settings</h3><div class="c row"><label>Mode <select id="mode"><option value="auto">Auto: trade when confident, ask when unsure</option><option value="ask">Ask me before every agent trade</option><option value="off">No new agent trades (exits still run)</option></select></label>
      <label>Agent size % <input id="size" type="number" min="1" max="20" value="${S.prefs.sizePct}" style="width:80px"></label><button class="pr" id="save">Save</button><small id="sv"></small></div>
    <p class="mu" style="margin-top:28px;font-size:13px">Paper-trading research demo. Fake money, simulated payments, no real orders, not investment advice. The backtest is net negative after costs, so expect losses more often than gains.</p>`;
    $("mode").value = S.prefs.mode;
  }

  function openDlg(kind) {
    const a = S.acct, px = a.lastPrice || 0, pos = (a.holdings || {}).NIFTYBEES;
    const head = kind === "fund" ? "Add money (simulated)" : kind + " NIFTYBEES";
    const body = kind === "fund"
      ? `<label>Amount (₹1,000 to ₹10,00,000)</label><input id="amt" type="number" min="1000" max="1000000" step="1000" value="50000"><label>Pay with</label><select id="meth"><option>UPI</option><option>Card</option><option>NetBanking</option></select><small class="mu">No real money moves. This only adds fake balance.</small>`
      : `<small class="mu">Last price ${inr(px)} · ${kind === "BUY" ? "Balance " + inr(a.cash) : "You hold " + (pos ? pos.qty : 0)}</small><label>Quantity</label><input id="qty" type="number" min="1" max="5000" value="1"><div id="est" class="mu" style="margin-top:6px"></div>`;
    dlg.innerHTML = `<b>${head}</b>${body}<label>Enter your PIN</label><input id="pin" type="password" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="6" autocomplete="off">
      <p class="row"><button class="${kind === "SELL" ? "sell" : kind === "BUY" ? "buy" : "pr"}" id="go">${kind === "fund" ? "Pay" : "Place " + kind.toLowerCase() + " order"}</button><button id="x">Close</button></p><small id="msg" class="n"></small>`;
    const est = () => { const q = +($("qty") || {}).value; if ($("est")) $("est").textContent = q ? "Estimated " + inr(q * px) : ""; };
    if ($("qty")) { $("qty").oninput = est; est(); }
  if (kind !== "fund") {
    const ad = advise(kind);
    dlg.querySelector("b").insertAdjacentHTML("afterend", `<div class="c" style="margin:10px 0"><b>Advisor: <span class="${ad.tone}">${esc(ad.stance)}</span></b><div>Suggested quantity: <b>${ad.qty}</b> ${ad.qty ? '<button type="button" id="use">Use</button>' : ""}</div><small>${ad.why.map(esc).join("<br>")}</small></div>`);
    if ($("use")) $("use").onclick = () => { $("qty").value = ad.qty; est(); };
  }
    $("x").onclick = () => dlg.close();
    $("go").onclick = async () => {
      const msg = $("msg"); msg.textContent = "";
      const pin = $("pin").value;
      if (!/^\d{4,6}$/.test(pin)) return msg.textContent = "Enter your 4 to 6 digit PIN.";
      $("go").disabled = true;
      try {
        const key = await derive(pin, S.salt);
        if (kind === "fund") {
          const amount = Math.round(+$("amt").value);
          if (!(amount >= 1000 && amount <= 1000000)) throw "Amount must be between ₹1,000 and ₹10,00,000.";
          const r = doc(collection(db, "accounts", U.uid, "fundreqs"));
          await setDoc(r, {amount, method: $("meth").value, status: "requested", proof: await proof(key, `${r.id}|FUND|${amount}`), createdAt: serverTimestamp()});
        } else {
          const qty = Math.round(+$("qty").value);
          if (!(qty >= 1 && qty <= 5000)) throw "Quantity must be between 1 and 5000.";
          if (kind === "BUY" && qty * px > a.cash) throw "Not enough balance.";
          if (kind === "SELL" && qty > (pos ? pos.qty : 0)) throw "You do not hold that many.";
          const r = doc(collection(db, "accounts", U.uid, "orders"));
          await setDoc(r, {side: kind, qty, status: "placed", proof: await proof(key, `${r.id}|${kind}|${qty}`), createdAt: serverTimestamp()});
        }
        dlg.close();
      } catch (e) { msg.textContent = typeof e === "string" ? e : "Could not submit: " + (e.code || e); $("go").disabled = false; }
    };
    dlg.showModal();
  }

  m.addEventListener("click", async e => {
    const b = e.target.closest("button"); if (!b) return;
    try {
      if (b.dataset.d) openDlg(b.dataset.d);
      else if (b.id === "open") {
        const nm = $("nm").value.trim(), p1 = $("p1").value, p2 = $("p2").value;
        if (nm.length < 2) return $("er").textContent = "Enter your name.";
        if (!/^\d{4,6}$/.test(p1)) return $("er").textContent = "PIN must be 4 to 6 digits.";
        if (p1 !== p2) return $("er").textContent = "PINs do not match.";
        b.disabled = true;
        await auth.currentUser.reload(); await auth.currentUser.getIdToken(true);
      const salt = hex(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16))), key = await derive(p1, salt);
        await setDoc(doc(db, "accounts", U.uid), {name: nm, cash: 1000000, startCash: 1000000, realized: 0, createdAt: serverTimestamp()});
        await setDoc(doc(db, "accounts", U.uid, "security", "salt"), {salt});
        await setDoc(doc(db, "accounts", U.uid, "security", "pin"), {key});
      } else if (b.dataset.id) { b.disabled = true; await updateDoc(doc(db, "accounts", U.uid, "pending", b.dataset.id), {status: b.dataset.s}); }
      else if (b.dataset.oc) await updateDoc(doc(db, "accounts", U.uid, "orders", b.dataset.oc), {status: "cancelled"});
      else if (b.id === "save") {
        await setDoc(doc(db, "accounts", U.uid, "settings", "prefs"), {mode: $("mode").value, sizePct: Number($("size").value)});
        $("sv").textContent = "Saved";
      }
    } catch (err) { alert("Could not do that: " + (err.code || err) + " | " + (err.message || "")); }
  });

  onAuthStateChanged(auth, u => {
    unsubs.forEach(f => f()); unsubs = []; U = u;
    if (!u) { m.innerHTML = '<div class="c">Sign in to see your paper account. <a href="subscribe.html" style="color:#6d9bff">Sign in or sign up</a></div>'; return; }
    const a = doc(db, "accounts", u.uid), on = (q, f) => unsubs.push(onSnapshot(q, f, err => console.warn(err.code)));
    const list = (c, k, n = 10) => on(query(collection(a, c), orderBy("createdAt", "desc"), limit(n)), s => { S[k] = s.docs.map(d => ({id: d.id, ...d.data()})); draw(); });
    on(a, s => { S.acct = s.exists() ? s.data() : null; draw(); });
    on(doc(a, "security", "salt"), s => { S.salt = s.exists() ? s.data().salt : null; });
    on(query(collection(a, "trades"), orderBy("ts", "desc"), limit(30)), s => { S.trades = s.docs.map(d => d.data()); draw(); });
    on(query(collection(a, "pending"), where("status", "==", "pending")), s => { S.pend = s.docs.map(d => ({id: d.id, ...d.data()})); draw(); });
    on(query(collection(a, "equity"), orderBy("date")), s => { S.eq = s.docs.map(d => d.data()); draw(); });
    on(doc(a, "settings", "prefs"), s => { if (s.exists()) S.prefs = s.data(); draw(); });
    list("orders", "ords"); list("fundreqs", "funds");
  });
  </script></body></html>

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">

<title>VERITAS Alerts</title>

  body{font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:520px;margin:24px auto;padding:0 16px;line-height:1.5}
  input[type=email],input[type=password]{width:100%;padding:10px;margin:6px 0;box-sizing:border-box}
  button{padding:10px 14px;margin:6px 6px 6px 0;cursor:pointer}
  .note{color:#555;font-size:13px} .msg{margin:10px 0;padding:8px;background:#f3f3f3;border-radius:6px}
  label{display:block;margin:8px 0}

<body>
<p><a href="./">&larr; Dashboard</a></p><h2>VERITAS paper-trading alerts</h2>
<p class="note">Educational paper-trading demo. No real orders are placed. Not investment advice.
Emails describe what a software model decided and why, including when it chose NOT to trade.</p>
<div id="msg" class="msg" hidden></div>

<div id="authBox">
  <input id="email" type="email" placeholder="Email" autocomplete="email">
  <input id="pw" type="password" placeholder="Password (min 6 chars)" autocomplete="current-password">
  <button id="signup">Sign up</button>
  <button id="login">Log in</button>
</div>

<div id="appBox" hidden>
  <p>Signed in as <b id="who"></b> &middot; email verified: <b id="ver"></b></p>
  <button id="resend" hidden>Resend verification email</button>
  <button id="refresh">I verified, refresh</button>
  <h3>Email preferences</h3>
  <label><input type="checkbox" id="optIn"> I agree to receive VERITAS paper-trading emails (you can untick this any time)</label>
  <label><input type="checkbox" id="digest" checked> Daily decision digest + evening recap</label>
  <label><input type="checkbox" id="instant" checked> Instant alerts (trade approved, red-team veto, VIX stress, safety stop)</label>
  <button id="save">Save preferences</button>
  <button id="logout">Log out</button>
</div>

<script type="module">
import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.14.1/firebase-app.js";
import { getAuth, createUserWithEmailAndPassword, signInWithEmailAndPassword,
         sendEmailVerification, onAuthStateChanged, signOut }
  from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.14.1/firebase-auth.js";
import { getFirestore, doc, getDoc, setDoc, serverTimestamp }
  from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/10.14.1/firebase-firestore.js";
import { firebaseConfig } from "./firebase-config.js";

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
const auth = getAuth(app);
const db = getFirestore(app);
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const say = (t) => { $("msg").textContent = t; $("msg").hidden = !t; };
const fail = (e) => say(e.code ? e.code.replace("auth/", "").replaceAll("-", " ") : String(e));

$("signup").onclick = async () => {
  try {
    const c = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, $("email").value.trim(), $("pw").value);
    await sendEmailVerification(c.user);
    say("Account created. Verification email sent. Check inbox and spam, then click 'I verified, refresh'.");
  } catch (e) { fail(e); }
};
$("login").onclick = async () => {
  try { await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, $("email").value.trim(), $("pw").value); say(""); }
  catch (e) { fail(e); }
};
$("logout").onclick = () => signOut(auth);
$("resend").onclick = async () => {
  try { await sendEmailVerification(auth.currentUser); say("Verification email sent again."); }
  catch (e) { fail(e); }
};
$("refresh").onclick = async () => { await auth.currentUser.reload(); await render(auth.currentUser); };
$("save").onclick = async () => {
  const u = auth.currentUser;
  if (!u.emailVerified && $("optIn").checked) { say("Verify your email first, then opt in."); return; }
  try {
    await setDoc(doc(db, "users", u.uid), {
      optIn: $("optIn").checked, digest: $("digest").checked,
      instant: $("instant").checked, updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });
    say($("optIn").checked ? "Saved. You will receive VERITAS paper-trading emails." : "Saved. You are unsubscribed.");
  } catch (e) { fail(e); }
};

async function render(u) {
  $("authBox").hidden = !!u; $("appBox").hidden = !u;
  if (!u) return;
  $("who").textContent = u.email;
  $("ver").textContent = u.emailVerified ? "yes" : "no";
  $("resend").hidden = u.emailVerified;
  const s = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", u.uid));
  const d = s.exists() ? s.data() : { optIn: false, digest: true, instant: true };
  $("optIn").checked = !!d.optIn; $("digest").checked = d.digest !== false; $("instant").checked = d.instant !== false;
}
onAuthStateChanged(auth, render);
</script>
</body>
</html>


===== FILE: ./worker/index.js =====
const ALLOW = [
  "https://veritas-trading.web.app",
  "https://veritas-trading.firebaseapp.com",
  "http://localhost:8000",
];
const hits = new Map();

const SYSTEM =
  "You are Ask VERITAS, a friendly assistant on a PAPER-trading " +
  "research dashboard. " +
  "1) For questions about VERITAS (decisions, dates, P&L, gates, " +
  "how it works) use ONLY the provided context. If a fact is not " +
  "in the context, say you don't have it. Never invent VERITAS " +
  "numbers. " +
  "2) For any other question (general knowledge, market concepts, " +
  "coding, anything) answer normally from your own knowledge. " +
  "3) Never give personal investment advice or tell the user to " +
  "buy or sell a real security. " +
  "Be simple and short. ALWAYS reply in English, even if the user " +
  "writes in romanized Marathi or Hindi (Hinglish). Reply in another " +
  "language only if the user explicitly asks for it or writes in " +
  "that language's own script.";

export default {
  async fetch(req, env) {
    const origin = req.headers.get("Origin") || "";
    const cors = {
      "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": ALLOW.includes(origin) ? origin : ALLOW[0],
      "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type",
      "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST,OPTIONS",
      "Vary": "Origin",
    };
    const json = (o, s = 200) =>
      new Response(JSON.stringify(o), {
        status: s,
        headers: { ...cors, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });

    if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: cors });
    if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "no" }, 405);
    if (!ALLOW.includes(origin)) return json({ error: "forbidden" }, 403);

    const ip = req.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") || "x";
    const now = Date.now();
    const arr = (hits.get(ip) || []).filter((t) => now - t < 60000);
    if (arr.length >= 15) {
      return json({ answer: "Too many questions, please wait a minute." });
    }
    arr.push(now);
    hits.set(ip, arr);
    if (hits.size > 5000) hits.clear();

    let b;
    try {
      b = await req.json();
    } catch (e) {
      return json({ error: "bad" }, 400);
    }
    const question = String(b.question || "").slice(0, 600);
    const ctx = JSON.stringify(b.context || {}).slice(0, 12000);
    const hist = Array.isArray(b.history) ? b.history.slice(-4) : [];

    const messages = [{ role: "system", content: SYSTEM }];
    for (const h of hist) {
      messages.push({ role: "user", content: String(h.q || "").slice(0, 300) });
      messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: String(h.a || "").slice(0, 500) });
    }
    messages.push({
      role: "user",
      content:
        "Question: " + question +
        "\n\nVERITAS context (use only for VERITAS questions):\n" + ctx,
    });

    try {
      const r = await fetch("https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": "Bearer " + env.GROQ_API_KEY,
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: "openai/gpt-oss-120b",
          temperature: 0.3,
          max_tokens: 900,
          reasoning_effort: "low",
          messages: messages,
        }),
      });
      const j = await r.json();
      const answer = j.choices && j.choices[0] && j.choices[0].message
        ? j.choices[0].message.content
        : null;
      return json({ answer: answer });
    } catch (e) {
      return json({ answer: null });
    }
  },
};


===== FILE: ./worker/wrangler.toml =====
name = "veritas-chat"
main = "index.js"
compatibility_date = "2025-01-01"
